\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
abstract base class for most VTK objects 

\doxylink{classvtk_object_base}{vtk\+Object\+Base} is the base class for all reference counted classes in the VTK. These classes include \doxylink{classvtk_command}{vtk\+Command} classes, \doxylink{classvtk_information_key}{vtk\+Information\+Key} classes, and \doxylink{classvtk_object}{vtk\+Object} classes.

\doxylink{classvtk_object_base}{vtk\+Object\+Base} performs reference counting:\+ objects that are reference counted exist as long as another object uses them. Once the last reference to a reference counted object is removed, the object will spontaneously destruct.

Constructor and destructor of the subclasses of \doxylink{classvtk_object_base}{vtk\+Object\+Base} should be protected, so that only \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_a53099f39f2279f95570e745237a52708}{New()} and \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_a2f77b7beccf178c43570367c50c41567}{Un\+Register()} actually call them. Debug leaks can be used to see if there are any objects left with nonzero reference count.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{Warning}
Note:\+ Objects of subclasses of \doxylink{classvtk_object_base}{vtk\+Object\+Base} should always be created with the \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_a53099f39f2279f95570e745237a52708}{New()} method and deleted with the \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_aa32595c1d2734908fe30344b77b77b32}{Delete()} method. They cannot be allocated off the stack (i.\+e., automatic objects) because the constructor is a protected method.
\end{DoxyWarning}

virtual void vtk\+Object\+Base::\+\+Delete (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Delete a VTK object. This method should always be used to delete an object when the \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_a53099f39f2279f95570e745237a52708}{New()} method was used to create it. Using the C++ delete method will not work with reference counting. \Hypertarget{classvtk_object_base_adc41d5233dba5974c1dd3cafc5008eae}\index{vtkObjectBase@{vtkObjectBase}!FastDelete@{FastDelete}}
\index{FastDelete@{FastDelete}!vtkObjectBase@{vtkObjectBase}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_object_base_adc41d5233dba5974c1dd3cafc5008eae} 
virtual void vtk\+Object\+Base::\+\+Fast\+Delete (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Delete a reference to this object. This version will not invoke garbage collection and can potentially leak the object if it is part of a reference loop. Use this method only when it is known that the object has another reference and would not be collected if a full garbage collection check were done. \Hypertarget{classvtk_object_base_af69b4fa99db0722a3025a3ba95d81052}\index{vtkObjectBase@{vtkObjectBase}!GetClassName@{GetClassName}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_object_base_acbcc0ffebe501f4cbb13b1fba32ecc45} 
virtual vtk\+Id\+Type vtk\+Object\+Base::\+\+Get\+Number\+Of\+Generations\+From\+Base (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{name}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Given the name of a base class of this class type, return the distance of inheritance between this class type and the named class (how many generations of inheritance are there between this class and the named class). If the named class is not in this class\textquotesingle{}s inheritance tree, return a negative value. Valid responses will always be nonnegative. This method works in combination with vtk\+Type\+Macro found in \doxylink{vtk_set_get_8h_source}{vtk\+Set\+Get.\+h}. \Hypertarget{classvtk_object_base_a1cafd6aa1e19cf0b791cba2b9bff43ef}\index{vtkObjectBase@{vtkObjectBase}!GetNumberOfGenerationsFromBaseType@{GetNumberOfGenerationsFromBaseType}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_object_base_a08b8a2d0047a4d9b787c4a822bcda456} 
virtual void vtk\+Object\+Base::\+\+Print\+Self (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{ostream \&}]{os}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{classvtk_indent}{vtk\+Indent}}}]{indent}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Methods invoked by print to print information about the object including superclasses. Typically not called by the user (use \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_aedadf6378465fd81c2592ef64262e2a5}{Print()} instead) but used in the hierarchical print process to combine the output of several classes.

\doxylink{requirements_The}{The} name of a directory, ideally mounted -\/o dax, to memory map an extended memory space within. This must be called before any objects are constructed in the extended space. Decrease the reference count (release by another object). This has the same effect as invoking \doxylink{classvtk_object_base_aa32595c1d2734908fe30344b77b77b32}{Delete()} (i.\+e., it reduces the reference count by 1).

\Hypertarget{classvtk_object_base_a93df39a29df633d0c79cb151151cfccd}\index{vtkObjectBase@{vtkObjectBase}!UsesGarbageCollector@{UsesGarbageCollector}}
\index{UsesGarbageCollector@{UsesGarbageCollector}!vtkObjectBase@{vtkObjectBase}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{UsesGarbageCollector()}{UsesGarbageCollector()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{classvtk_object_base_a93df39a29df633d0c79cb151151cfccd} 
virtual bool vtk\+Object\+Base::\+\+Uses\+Garbage\+Collector (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption}) const\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [virtual]}}

Indicate whether the class uses {\ttfamily \doxylink{classvtk_garbage_collector}{vtk\+Garbage\+Collector}} or not.

Most classes will not need to do this, but if the class participates in a strongly-\/connected reference count cycle, participation can resolve these cycles.

If overriding this method to return true, the {\ttfamily Report\+References} method should be overridden to report references that may be in cycles.
